<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">01</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.anthropic.com/news/statement-department-of-war" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Statement from Dario Amodei on our discussions with the Department of War</span><span class='zh'>阿莫代伊就与国防部的讨论发表声明</span></a><span class="dom">anthropic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Dario Amodei addresses discussions with the Department of War, clarifying the scope and boundaries of conversations around AI safety and defense applications.</span><span class='zh'>阿莫代伊就与国防部的讨论发表声明，澄清对话的边界和安全议题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Dario Amodei addresses discussions with the Department of War, clarifying the scope and boundaries of conversations around AI safety and defense applications. He emphasizes responsible collaboration and ongoing dialogue with policymakers. This matters for industry trust and shaping policy directions.</span><span class='zh'>阿莫代伊就与国防部的讨论发表声明，澄清对话的边界和安全议题。强调负责任的协作、治理以及与决策者的持续对话。这对行业信任和政策走向具有一定影响。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A compact framework for running GPT-like agents on modest hardware.</span><span class='zh'>提出在中等算力设备上运行 GPT 风格代理的紧凑框架。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A compact framework for running GPT-like agents on modest hardware. Emphasizes modular tooling, lightweight memory, and efficient orchestration to enable on-device AI and edge applications. This approach could lower barriers to deploying autonomous AI in real-world products.</span><span class='zh'>提出在中等算力设备上运行 GPT 风格代理的紧凑框架。强调模块化工具、轻量级内存和高效编排，以实现边缘设备上的 AI 自主执行。该思路可能降低在真实产品中部署自治 AI 的门槛。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">06</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://spectrum.ieee.org/age-verification" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Age Verification Trap: Verifying age undermines everyone&#x27;s data protection</span><span class='zh'>年龄验证陷阱：核验年龄削弱每个人的数据保护</span></a><span class="dom">spectrum.ieee.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Mandatory age checks often force users to reveal IDs or pass through centralized validators, increasing data exposure.</span><span class='zh'>强制年龄核验往往要求用户提供身份信息或通过集中验证服务进行，显著增加数据暴露风险，削弱既有的数据保护保障，并带来新的被滥用与被攻破的风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Mandatory age checks often force users to reveal IDs or pass through centralized validators, increasing data exposure. This undermines established data-protection guarantees and creates new attack surfaces for breaches and misuse.</span><span class='zh'>强制年龄核验往往要求用户提供身份信息或通过集中验证服务进行，显著增加数据暴露风险，削弱既有的数据保护保障，并带来新的被滥用与被攻破的风险。文章强调隐私设计的重要性，呼吁采用更具隐私保护的替代方案。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">07</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://hawksley.org/2026/02/17/timeframe.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I built Timeframe, our family e-paper dashboard</span><span class='zh'>Timeframe，我们家的电子纸仪表板</span></a><span class="dom">hawksley.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Timeframe is a family e-paper dashboard that runs on a low-power display for glanceable updates.</span><span class='zh'>Timeframe 是一个用于家庭的电子纸仪表板，利用低功耗显示实现一眼可览的更新。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Timeframe is a family e-paper dashboard that runs on a low-power display for glanceable updates. It aggregates calendar, weather, and household notes, offering a less distracting alternative to smartphone widgets.</span><span class='zh'>Timeframe 是一个用于家庭的电子纸仪表板，利用低功耗显示实现一眼可览的更新。它整合日历、天气和家务简报等信息，提供比手机通知更少干扰的主页式信息源。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/sama/status/2027578652477821175" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>OpenAI agrees with Dept. of War to deploy models in their classified network</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 与战争部在其机密网络中部署模型达成协议</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>OpenAI reaches an agreement to deploy its models inside the War Department&#x27;s classified network, marking a rare government-use approval for advanced AI.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 达成在战争部机密网络中部署其模型的协议，标志着在高敏环境中对先进 AI 的罕见应用获批。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>OpenAI reaches an agreement to deploy its models inside the War Department&#x27;s classified network, marking a rare government-use approval for advanced AI. The deal raises questions about security, vendor oversight, and the scale of AI integration into sensitive environments. It signals a potential path for enterprise and defense collaborations, with tight governance requirements.</span><span class='zh'>OpenAI 达成在战争部机密网络中部署其模型的协议，标志着在高敏环境中对先进 AI 的罕见应用获批。该交易引发对安全、供应商监管以及在敏感场景中部署 AI 的规模等问题的讨论。它预示着企业与防务领域日后更紧密的协作之路，同时将伴随严格的治理要求。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://twitter.com/secwar/status/2027507717469049070" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>I am directing the Department of War to designate Anthropic a supply-chain risk</span><span class='zh'>我指示战争部将 Anthropic 列为供应链风险</span></a><span class="dom">twitter.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The Department of War reportedly intends to designate Anthropic as a supply-chain risk, which could curb its access to sensitive government projects and influence how agencies screen AI vendors.</span><span class='zh'>据称战争部计划将 Anthropic 列为供应链风险，可能限制其参与敏感政府项目并影响机构对 AI 供应商的评估。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The Department of War reportedly intends to designate Anthropic as a supply-chain risk, which could curb its access to sensitive government projects and influence how agencies screen AI vendors. The move underscores growing government scrutiny of AI suppliers and the need for robust security practices.</span><span class='zh'>据称战争部计划将 Anthropic 列为供应链风险，可能限制其参与敏感政府项目并影响机构对 AI 供应商的评估。这体现政府对 AI 供应商日益严格的审查与对安全实践的强调。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Steps to cancel ChatGPT subscriptions are documented in help; you can navigate to account settings, manage subscription, and confirm cancellation.</span><span class='zh'>帮助文档列出取消 ChatGPT 订阅的具体步骤：进入账户设置、管理订阅并确认取消。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Steps to cancel ChatGPT subscriptions are documented in help; you can navigate to account settings, manage subscription, and confirm cancellation. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.pcgamer.com/software/operating-systems/a-new-california-law-says-all-operating-systems-including-linux-need-to-have-some-form-of-age-verification-at-account-setup/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>A new California law says all operating systems need to have age verification</span><span class='zh'>加州新法规定所有操作系统在设置账户时需进行年龄验证</span></a><span class="dom">pcgamer.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>California passes a law requiring age verification at OS setup for all devices, including Linux.</span><span class='zh'>加州通过新法，要求所有操作系统在账户设置时进行年龄验证，覆盖包括 Linux 在内的设备。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>California passes a law requiring age verification at OS setup for all devices, including Linux. The move aims to curb underage access but could complicate privacy and OSS distribution, forcing platforms to implement new authentication flows.</span><span class='zh'>加州通过新法，要求所有操作系统在账户设置时进行年龄验证，覆盖包括 Linux 在内的设备。此举旨在限制未成年人使用，同时可能带来隐私与开源软件分发的合规挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues against purchasing a .online domain due to branding, trust, and SEO drawbacks, plus the risk of confusion with similar domains.</span><span class='zh'>本文劝说读者不要购买 .online 域名，理由包括品牌信任、搜索引擎优化和长期维护成本等，以及与同类域名的混淆风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues against purchasing a .online domain due to branding, trust, and SEO drawbacks, plus the risk of confusion with similar domains. It urges choosing a more reputable TLD for lasting credibility.</span><span class='zh'>本文劝说读者不要购买 .online 域名，理由包括品牌信任、搜索引擎优化和长期维护成本等，以及与同类域名的混淆风险。建议选择更具信誉的顶级域名以建立长期信任。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://techcrunch.com/2026/02/23/americans-are-destroying-flock-surveillance-cameras/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Americans are destroying Flock surveillance cameras</span><span class='zh'>美国人正在破坏 Flock 的监控摄像头</span></a><span class="dom">techcrunch.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Rising privacy activism leads individuals to vandalize or remove license-plate cameras from a private vendor, highlighting tensions between public safety tech and civil liberties.</span><span class='zh'>随着隐私行动主义的兴起，个人对监控设备的破坏和撤下行为增多，凸显公共安全技术与公民自由之间的张力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Rising privacy activism leads individuals to vandalize or remove license-plate cameras from a private vendor, highlighting tensions between public safety tech and civil liberties. The incident underscores how consumer-privacy backlash can impact surveillance infrastructure and vendor deployments. It raises questions about the resilience and governance of city-scale surveillance programs.</span><span class='zh'>随着隐私行动主义的兴起，个人对监控设备的破坏和撤下行为增多，凸显公共安全技术与公民自由之间的张力。事件暴露出执法与商业监控基础设施在治理、韧性与隐私保护上的挑战。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ghostty.org/docs" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Ghostty – Terminal Emulator</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 终端模拟器</span></a><span class="dom">ghostty.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Ghostty presents a lightweight terminal emulator focused on fast rendering and a modern UX.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 是一个轻量级的终端模拟器，关注快速渲染和现代用户体验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Ghostty presents a lightweight terminal emulator focused on fast rendering and a modern UX. It showcases how a lean terminal app can balance UTF-8, color support, and cross-platform compatibility without feature bloat.</span><span class='zh'>Ghostty 是一个轻量级的终端模拟器，关注快速渲染和现代用户体验。它展示了一个简洁的终端应用在对 UTF-8、颜色显示以及跨平台兼容性之间的平衡，避免功能臃肿。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://susam.net/attention-media-vs-social-networks.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Attention Media ≠ Social Networks</span><span class='zh'>注意力媒体≠社交网络</span></a><span class="dom">susam.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Media attention and social networks are not the same thing; attention economics often treats content discovery and platform design differently.</span><span class='zh'>媒体关注度与社交网络并非同一概念；注意力经济往往把内容发现与平台设计区分对待。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Media attention and social networks are not the same thing; attention economics often treats content discovery and platform design differently. The piece argues for designing systems that measure and monetize attention without conflating it with the social graph.</span><span class='zh'>媒体关注度与社交网络并非同一概念；注意力经济往往把内容发现与平台设计区分对待。该文主张在衡量和变现注意力时，避免与社交关系网混为一谈。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A court held that the Fourth Amendment does not broadly authorize searching protesters’ devices, marking a significant privacy protection for digital evidence.</span><span class='zh'>法院裁定第四修正案不支持对抗议者设备的广泛搜索，强化了数字隐私保护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A court held that the Fourth Amendment does not broadly authorize searching protesters’ devices, marking a significant privacy protection for digital evidence. The ruling narrows police power to seize or inspect personal data at demonstrations.</span><span class='zh'>法院裁定第四修正案不支持对抗议者设备的广泛搜索，强化了数字隐私保护。这一裁决缩小了警方在示威现场查验个人数据的权限。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ewtnnews.com/vatican/pope-leo-xiv-tells-priests-to-use-their-brains-not-ai-to-write-homilies" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Pope tells priests to use their brains, not AI, to write homilies</span><span class='zh'>教皇让神父用脑子而非AI来写讲道</span></a><span class="dom">ewtnnews.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Pope Francis urges priests to rely on human judgment rather than AI to craft homilies, signaling cautious boundaries for AI in sacred duties.</span><span class='zh'>教皇方济各呼吁神父在撰写讲道时优先运用人类判断，而非依赖 AI，标志着在宗教敏感工作中对 AI 的审慎边界。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Pope Francis urges priests to rely on human judgment rather than AI to craft homilies, signaling cautious boundaries for AI in sacred duties. The stance reflects broader debates about automation&#x27;s role in culturally sensitive work.</span><span class='zh'>教皇方济各呼吁神父在撰写讲道时优先运用人类判断，而非依赖 AI，标志着在宗教敏感工作中对 AI 的审慎边界。其观点映射出自动化在文化性强工作中的广泛辩论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Whistleblowers reportedly flagged a large crypto transfer to Iran, prompting Binance to terminate the employees who raised the concern.</span><span class='zh'>内部举报者据称发现有价值1.7亿美元的加密资金流向伊朗，随后被币安解雇。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Whistleblowers reportedly flagged a large crypto transfer to Iran, prompting Binance to terminate the employees who raised the concern. It also raises questions about how exchanges monitor flows and protect employees who surface potential violations.</span><span class='zh'>内部举报者据称发现有价值1.7亿美元的加密资金流向伊朗，随后被币安解雇。事件凸显合规、制裁规避与对内部举报者的保护之间的难题，也引发关于交易所如何监控资金流与保护员工的讨论。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The US is pushing diplomats to push back on data sovereignty laws worldwide, arguing they hinder cross-border data flows and national interests.</span><span class='zh'>美国推动外交官抵制全球范围内的数据主权法案，声称此类法规阻碍跨境数据流动并影响国家利益。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The US is pushing diplomats to push back on data sovereignty laws worldwide, arguing they hinder cross-border data flows and national interests. The move highlights the ongoing policy clash over where data should reside and who can access it.</span><span class='zh'>美国推动外交官抵制全球范围内的数据主权法案，声称此类法规阻碍跨境数据流动并影响国家利益。此举凸显了数据应存放在哪、谁有权访问数据等方面的政策博弈。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>This demo imagines a free, ad supported AI chat and highlights how revenue models could shape latency, limits, and personalization.</span><span class='zh'>这个演示设想了免费且由广告支持的 AI 聊天，揭示了收入模式如何影响响应时延、使用限制和个性化。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>This demo imagines a free, ad supported AI chat and highlights how revenue models could shape latency, limits, and personalization. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.rockoder.com/beyondthecode/cognitive-debt-when-velocity-exceeds-comprehension/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cognitive Debt: When Velocity Exceeds Comprehension</span><span class='zh'>認知債務：當速度超過理解力</span></a><span class="dom">rockoder.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that pushing development velocity too hard creates cognitive debt—hidden mental load and architectural debt that slows future work.</span><span class='zh'>文章認為過度追求開發速度會產生認知債務，即藏匿的心理負擔與架構負擔，最終拖慢未來的工作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that pushing development velocity too hard creates cognitive debt—hidden mental load and architectural debt that slows future work. It promotes better onboarding, documentation, and mental models to keep velocity sustainable. The takeaway: speed must be matched with clarity.</span><span class='zh'>文章認為過度追求開發速度會產生認知債務，即藏匿的心理負擔與架構負擔，最終拖慢未來的工作。建議透過更好的上手指引、文件與心智模型，讓速度持續可行。核心觀點是：速度需要與清晰度並行。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://mlu-explain.github.io/decision-tree/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Decision trees – the unreasonable power of nested decision rules</span><span class='zh'>决策树：嵌套决策规则的强大力量</span></a><span class="dom">mlu-explain.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy.</span><span class='zh'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Nested decision rules let a decision tree encode nuanced logic with transparent paths, but depth can make them unwieldy. With careful structure and pruning, they remain a practical tool for governable, explainable rules even as data-driven models rise.</span><span class='zh'>嵌套决策规则使决策树能够以透明的路径表达细致的逻辑，但深度增加会使其变得难以维护。通过合适的结构和剪枝，它仍然是可解释性强、可治理的规则工具，即便在数据驱动模型盛行时也有用。</span></p>

The release also brings several performance and privacy enhancements, reflecting Mozilla&#x27;s stance on user autonomy in an AI-enabled browser.</span><span class='zh'>Firefox 148 引入“AI 取消开关”，让用户在浏览器中的 AI 功能可控，回应隐私与信任问题。同时还带来多项性能与隐私改进，体现 Mozilla 在 AI 圈定用户自主权的立场。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://keepandroidopen.org/open-letter/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Open Letter to Google on Mandatory Developer Registration for App Distribution</span><span class='zh'>致 Google 的公开信：应用分发需强制开发者注册</span></a><span class="dom">keepandroidopen.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A public letter urges Google to rethink mandatory developer registration for app distribution, arguing it could hamper innovation, transparency, and developer autonomy.</span><span class='zh'>公开信呼吁 Google 重新考虑应用分发的强制开发者注册，指其可能削弱创新与开发者自治，主张更开放、隐私友好的分发方式。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A public letter urges Google to rethink mandatory developer registration for app distribution, arguing it could hamper innovation, transparency, and developer autonomy. The authors advocate more open, privacy-conscious approaches.</span><span class='zh'>公开信呼吁 Google 重新考虑应用分发的强制开发者注册，指其可能削弱创新与开发者自治，主张更开放、隐私友好的分发方式。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Alibaba&#x27;s Qwen3.5 122B and 35B variants achieve Sonnet 4.5-level performance on local hardware, marking a notable efficiency gain for open-source LLMs.</span><span class='zh'>阿里巴巴的 Qwen3.5 122B 与 35B 模型在本地硬件上实现了接近 Sonnet 4.5 的性能，标志着开源大型语言模型的显著效率提升。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://stackoverflow.com/questions/818255/what-does-21-mean" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>What does &quot; 2&gt;&amp;1 &quot; mean?</span><span class='zh'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; 的含义是什么？</span></a><span class="dom">stackoverflow.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; merges standard error and output streams in shells, a fundamental redirection technique.</span><span class='zh'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; 将标准错误输出合并到标准输出，常用于日志和管道中的输出控制。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; merges standard error and output streams in shells, a fundamental redirection technique. It covers common use cases, caveats, and how it behaves in pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>&quot;2&gt;&amp;1&quot; 将标准错误输出合并到标准输出，常用于日志和管道中的输出控制。说明了基本用法、常见场景以及潜在的坑。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A security misconfiguration allowed one person to control around 7,000 robot vacuums, exposing IoT risk at scale.</span><span class='zh'>一次安全配置错误让一人能够控制约7000台机器人吸尘器，暴露了大规模物联网风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A security misconfiguration allowed one person to control around 7,000 robot vacuums, exposing IoT risk at scale. The incident underscores why strong authentication, firmware hardening, and vendor safeguards matter for consumer devices.</span><span class='zh'>一次安全配置错误让一人能够控制约7000台机器人吸尘器，暴露了大规模物联网风险。事件强调了强认证、固件加固和厂商防护对消费级设备的重要性。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AirSnitch demonstrates practical methods to defeat client isolation in Wi‑Fi networks, revealing attack vectors.</span><span class='zh'>AirSnitch 的研究揭示了 Wi‑Fi 客户端隔离的可被利用的缺口，指出了改进接入点隔离和其他缓解措施的必要性。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AirSnitch demonstrates practical methods to defeat client isolation in Wi‑Fi networks, revealing attack vectors. The work motivates stronger AP isolation and mitigations, underscoring ongoing security concerns in wireless deployments.</span><span class='zh'>AirSnitch 的研究揭示了 Wi‑Fi 客户端隔离的可被利用的缺口，指出了改进接入点隔离和其他缓解措施的必要性。对无线网络安全的警示意义明显。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cheaper sub-$200 solid-state lidar could reshape the economics of automotive sensing, lowering per-vehicle sensor costs and accelerating ADAS adoption.</span><span class='zh'>成本低廉的固态激光雷达可能重塑汽车传感器的经济格局，显著降低单车传感器成本并推动高级驾驶辅助系统的普及。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cheaper sub-$200 solid-state lidar could reshape the economics of automotive sensing, lowering per-vehicle sensor costs and accelerating ADAS adoption. But manufacturers still face hurdles in performance, reliability, and scalable production that will determine how quickly the market shifts.</span><span class='zh'>成本低廉的固态激光雷达可能重塑汽车传感器的经济格局，显著降低单车传感器成本并推动高级驾驶辅助系统的普及。然而要实现大规模生产，性能、可靠性与产能扩张仍是关键挑战。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>ASML reveals a next-generation EUV light source that could boost chip yields by up to 50% by 2030, easing the roadmap pressure for leading-node fabrication.</span><span class='zh'>ASML 公布下一代 EUV 光源技术，若商用可实现到2030年产出提升约50%，将缓解先进制程的产能压力并推动成本下降。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>ASML reveals a next-generation EUV light source that could boost chip yields by up to 50% by 2030, easing the roadmap pressure for leading-node fabrication. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://hacks.mozilla.org/2026/02/goodbye-innerhtml-hello-sethtml-stronger-xss-protection-in-firefox-148/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Goodbye InnerHTML, Hello SetHTML: Stronger XSS Protection in Firefox 148</span><span class='zh'>告别 InnerHTML，迎来 SetHTML：Firefox 148 提升 XSS 防护</span></a><span class="dom">hacks.mozilla.org</span></h3>

<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Firefox 148 introduces SetHTML for safer handling of HTML to reduce XSS risks, moving away from innerHTML in many contexts. <h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonwillison.net/guides/agentic-engineering-patterns/code-is-cheap/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Writing code is cheap now</span><span class='zh'>写代码现在很便宜</span></a><span class="dom">simonwillison.net</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cheap code is accelerating prototyping and experimentation as AI copilots handle boilerplate and refactoring.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 助手接手大量模板化与重复性编码，写代码成本显著降低，开发速度加快。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cheap code is accelerating prototyping and experimentation as AI copilots handle boilerplate and refactoring. The piece argues this lowers barriers for building, but warns that maintainability and security still require strong design — new patterns like agentic engineering emerge to manage complexity.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 助手接手大量模板化与重复性编码，写代码成本显著降低，开发速度加快。文章提醒人们，尽管成本下降，代码质量、维护性和安全性仍需依赖良好设计，并提出将代理式工程等新模式用于管理复杂性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://opper.ai/blog/car-wash-test" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>“Car Wash” test with 53 models</span><span class='zh'>“Car Wash”测试覆盖53种模型</span></a><span class="dom">opper.ai</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The &#x27;Car Wash&#x27; test benchmark evaluates 53 machine learning models, highlighting differences in robustness under common refactoring or input perturbations.</span><span class='zh'>“Car Wash”测试对53个模型进行了评估，凸显在常见扰动下的鲁棒性差异。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The &#x27;Car Wash&#x27; test benchmark evaluates 53 machine learning models, highlighting differences in robustness under common refactoring or input perturbations. The results illustrate how model quality varies across architectures and training regimes, stressing the need for robust evaluation.</span><span class='zh'>“Car Wash”测试对53个模型进行了评估，凸显在常见扰动下的鲁棒性差异。结果揭示了不同架构和训练策略的模型在质量上的差异，强调需要更稳健的评估标准。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://ejholmes.github.io/2026/02/28/mcp-is-dead-long-live-the-cli.html" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>When does MCP make sense vs CLI?</span><span class='zh'>MCP 何时有意义，CLI 何时更合适？</span></a><span class="dom">ejholmes.github.io</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that MCP is largely obsolete, while the CLI remains the flexible and reliable tool for automation.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为 MCP 基本过时，CLI 仍然是自动化的灵活且可信赖的工具。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that MCP is largely obsolete, while the CLI remains the flexible and reliable tool for automation. It contrasts scenarios where a higher-level protocol might be tempting against the practicality and ecosystem support of the CLI. Takeaway: design tooling with a CLI-first mindset and reserve MCP for niche orchestration tasks.</span><span class='zh'>文章认为 MCP 基本过时，CLI 仍然是自动化的灵活且可信赖的工具。对比了在需要重复编排时 MCP 可能有用的情景与 CLI 的可用性和生态系统优势。核心观点是优先以 CLI 为中心设计工具，MCP 仅在少数专业编排任务中才有价值。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.ivanturkovic.com/2026/02/25/ai-made-writing-code-easier-engineering-harder/" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>AI Made Writing Code Easier. It Made Being an Engineer Harder</span><span class='zh'>AI 让写代码更容易，却让工程师更难</span></a><span class="dom">ivanturkovic.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>AI can accelerate code generation and boilerplate, but it shifts the burden to engineers for design, vetting, and integration.</span><span class='zh'>AI 在生成代码和处理重复任务方面提升了效率，但也把设计、审核与整合的责任压在工程师身上。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>AI can accelerate code generation and boilerplate, but it shifts the burden to engineers for design, vetting, and integration. The result is higher cognitive load and accountability as teams balance speed with reliability, security, and maintainability.</span><span class='zh'>AI 在生成代码和处理重复任务方面提升了效率，但也把设计、审核与整合的责任压在工程师身上。工作强度和对输出质量的要求提高，需要在速度、可靠性与安全性之间取得平衡。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://www.lesswrong.com/posts/8m6AM5qtPMjgTkEeD/my-journey-to-the-microwave-alternate-timeline" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>My journey to the microwave alternate timeline</span><span class='zh'>我通往微波炉的平行时间线之旅</span></a><span class="dom">lesswrong.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A reflective piece that uses a hypothetical journey to a microwave as a stand-in for exploring alternate timelines and the consequences of small tech decisions.</span><span class='zh'>这是一篇个人随笔，以微波炉作为隐喻，探讨如何通过日常选择进入不同的时间线。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A reflective piece that uses a hypothetical journey to a microwave as a stand-in for exploring alternate timelines and the consequences of small tech decisions.</span><span class='zh'>这是一篇个人随笔，以微波炉作为隐喻，探讨如何通过日常选择进入不同的时间线。作者反思理性决策与科技进展对人生轨迹的影响。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://simonlermen.substack.com/p/large-scale-online-deanonymization" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Large-Scale Online Deanonymization with LLMs</span><span class='zh'>大规模在线去匿名化与大语言模型</span></a><span class="dom">simonlermen.substack.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The piece argues that large language models, combined with publicly available data, enable dramatic scale in online deanonymization, raising privacy and security concerns.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，结合大型语言模型和公开数据，能够在大规模层面开展在线去匿名化，引发隐私与安全担忧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The piece argues that large language models, combined with publicly available data, enable dramatic scale in online deanonymization, raising privacy and security concerns. It discusses potential attack surfaces and mitigation strategies, highlighting the tension between AI capabilities and individual anonymity on the open web.</span><span class='zh'>文章指出，结合大型语言模型和公开数据，能够在大规模层面开展在线去匿名化，引发隐私与安全担忧。分析了可能的攻击面与缓解策略，强调了AI能力与个人隐私保护之间的矛盾。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://dl.acm.org/doi/fullHtml/10.1145/238386.238611" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>The Windows 95 user interface: A case study in usability engineering (1996)</span><span class='zh'>Windows 95 用户界面：可用性工程案例研究（1996）</span></a><span class="dom">dl.acm.org</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>An early look at how Windows 95&#x27;s UI benefited from usability engineering, including user testing, task-focused layouts, and predictable navigation.</span><span class='zh'>早期对 Windows 95 用户界面的分析，揭示其在可用性工程方面的实践，如用户测试、以任务为中心的布局和可预测的导航。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>An early look at how Windows 95&#x27;s UI benefited from usability engineering, including user testing, task-focused layouts, and predictable navigation. The case demonstrates the value of iterative feedback for complex desktop interfaces, underscoring ongoing trade-offs between feature richness and learnability.</span><span class='zh'>早期对 Windows 95 用户界面的分析，揭示其在可用性工程方面的实践，如用户测试、以任务为中心的布局和可预测的导航。该案例展示了迭代反馈在处理复杂桌面界面中的价值，并强调功能丰富与易学易用之间的权衡。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>PgDog offers a method to scale PostgreSQL capacity without requiring changes to client apps, potentially via a sharding/proxy approach.</span><span class='zh'>PgDog 提供一种在不修改客户端应用的前提下扩大 PostgreSQL 容量的方法，可能通过代理层或分片实现。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>PgDog offers a method to scale PostgreSQL capacity without requiring changes to client apps, potentially via a sharding/proxy approach. If effective, it could simplify scaling for existing deployments and reduce migration risk.</span><span class='zh'>PgDog 提供一种在不修改客户端应用的前提下扩大 PostgreSQL 容量的方法，可能通过代理层或分片实现。若有效，将简化现有部署的扩容工作，降低迁移风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/anadim/AdderBoard" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Smallest transformer that can add two 10-digit numbers</span><span class='zh'>能够把两组十位数相加的最小 Transformer</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A minimalist Transformer architecture demonstrates it can reliably add two 10-digit integers, illustrating the surprising arithmetic capability of language models.</span><span class='zh'>一个极简的 Transformer 架构能够可靠地把两组十位整数相加，展示了语言模型在算术任务上的意外能力。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A minimalist Transformer architecture demonstrates it can reliably add two 10-digit integers, illustrating the surprising arithmetic capability of language models. The project, AdderBoard, benchmarks how small a model can be to perform precise arithmetic and hints at why transformer-based reasoning can be surprisingly efficient on narrow tasks. It also invites discussion on hardware constraints and reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>一个极简的 Transformer 架构能够可靠地把两组十位整数相加，展示了语言模型在算术任务上的意外能力。该项目 AdderBoard 评估了多小的模型就能完成精确计算，并就 transformer 推理在特定任务中的效率展开讨论。也引发对硬件约束与可复现性的思考。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://gist.github.com/dollspace-gay/d8d3bc3ecf4188df049d7a4726bb2a00" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Verified Spec-Driven Development (VSDD)</span><span class='zh'>经验证的规范驱动开发（VSDD）</span></a><span class="dom">gist.github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Verified Spec-Driven Development advocates anchoring software work to verified specifications, then deriving tests and implementations from them.</span><span class='zh'>经验证的规范驱动开发主张以经过验证的规范为基石，进而推导测试与实现，强调保持规范的准确性、促成团队对齐并降低误解风险。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Verified Spec-Driven Development advocates anchoring software work to verified specifications, then deriving tests and implementations from them. It emphasizes keeping specs honest, aligning teams, and reducing misinterpretation.</span><span class='zh'>经验证的规范驱动开发主张以经过验证的规范为基石，进而推导测试与实现，强调保持规范的准确性、促成团队对齐并降低误解风险。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/tempestphp/100-million-row-challenge" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>100M-Row Challenge with PHP</span><span class='zh'>用 PHP 实现一亿行数据挑战</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>The 100M-Row Challenge tests PHP&#x27;s data processing paths at scale, exploring memory usage, streaming versus loading, and practical tricks to handle huge datasets.</span><span class='zh'>100M 行挑战测试 PHP 在大规模数据处理中的内存、流式与加载策略等瓶颈，并给出提升性能的实用技巧。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>The 100M-Row Challenge tests PHP&#x27;s data processing paths at scale, exploring memory usage, streaming versus loading, and practical tricks to handle huge datasets. The project offers benchmarks and lessons for PHP developers facing large-scale data workloads.</span><span class='zh'>100M 行挑战测试 PHP 在大规模数据处理中的内存、流式与加载策略等瓶颈，并给出提升性能的实用技巧。该项目为面向海量数据的 PHP 开发者提供基准和经验。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mandel-macaque/memento" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>If AI writes code, should the session be part of the commit?</span><span class='zh'>如果 AI 写代码，会话应该成为提交的一部分吗？</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>As AI-assisted coding becomes common, the question arises whether interactive sessions—prompts, tool outputs, and environment state—should be captured in version control.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 辅助写码的普及，交互会话（提示、工具输出与环境状态）是否应被记录在版本控制中成为一个问题。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>As AI-assisted coding becomes common, the question arises whether interactive sessions—prompts, tool outputs, and environment state—should be captured in version control. The project discusses approaches to embedding or separating session metadata from code commits to aid reproducibility.</span><span class='zh'>随着 AI 辅助写码的普及，交互会话（提示、工具输出与环境状态）是否应被记录在版本控制中成为一个问题。该项目探讨将会话元数据嵌入提交或与代码分离的做法，以提升可复现性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/JeffBezanson/femtolisp" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>femtolisp: A lightweight, robust, scheme-like Lisp implementation</span><span class='zh'>femtolisp：一种轻量、稳健的 Scheme 风格 Lisp 实现</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>femtolisp is a compact Lisp implementation inspired by Scheme, prioritizing small footprint and robust semantics for embedding or experimentation.</span><span class='zh'>femtolisp 是一个受 Scheme 启发的轻量 Lisp 实现，关注极小体积与稳健语义，适合嵌入式场景和语言实验。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>femtolisp is a compact Lisp implementation inspired by Scheme, prioritizing small footprint and robust semantics for embedding or experimentation. It offers a pleasant sandbox for language enthusiasts to explore Lisp VM design.</span><span class='zh'>femtolisp 是一个受 Scheme 启发的轻量 Lisp 实现，关注极小体积与稳健语义，适合嵌入式场景和语言实验。为语言爱好者提供了一个探索 Lisp 虚拟机设计的便携环境。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/atgreen/cl-kawa" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Cl-kawa: Scheme on Java on Common Lisp</span><span class='zh'>cl-kawa：用 Common Lisp 在 Java 平台上实现 Scheme</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Cl-kawa implements Scheme on the JVM using Common Lisp, enabling Scheme code to run alongside Java with interop to Java libraries.</span><span class='zh'>cl-kawa 让 Scheme 能在 JVM 上运行，借助 Common Lisp 的实现实现与 Java 的互操作。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Cl-kawa implements Scheme on the JVM using Common Lisp, enabling Scheme code to run alongside Java with interop to Java libraries. The project highlights the challenges of hosting a Lisp dialect on the Java platform and offers a path for language experimentation on the JVM.</span><span class='zh'>cl-kawa 让 Scheme 能在 JVM 上运行，借助 Common Lisp 的实现实现与 Java 的互操作。该工作揭示在 JVM 上混合 Lisp 方言的挑战与可能性，为语言实验和跨语言开发提供了有趣的路径。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/wkoszek/emuko" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Emuko: Fast RISC-V emulator written in Rust, boots Linux</span><span class='zh'>Emuko：用 Rust 编写的快速 RISC-V 模拟器，能够启动 Linux</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Emuko is a high-performance RISC-V emulator written in Rust that can boot a Linux kernel, a notable achievement for systems emulation in safe languages.</span><span class='zh'>Emuko 是用 Rust 编写的高性能 RISC-V 模拟器，能够引导 Linux 内核，是在安全语言中实现系统仿真的重要成就。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Emuko is a high-performance RISC-V emulator written in Rust that can boot a Linux kernel, a notable achievement for systems emulation in safe languages. It provides a practical platform for experimenting with OS internals and virtualization ideas without the overhead of full hardware. The project showcases Rust’s viability for low-level tooling.</span><span class='zh'>Emuko 是用 Rust 编写的高性能 RISC-V 模拟器，能够引导 Linux 内核，是在安全语言中实现系统仿真的重要成就。它为研究操作系统内部结构和虚拟化思路提供了一个实际平台，避免了直接硬件的开销。该项目展示了 Rust 作为底层工具的可行性。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/cosmiciron/vmprint" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: I built a zero-browser, pure-JS typesetting engine for bit-perfect PDFs</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：零浏览器、纯 JavaScript 的位精确排版引擎</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>A pure-JS, zero-dependency typesetting engine that outputs bit-perfect PDFs without relying on a browser&#x27;s layout engine.</span><span class='zh'>一个纯 JavaScript、零依赖的排版引擎，输出位精确的 PDF，不依赖浏览器排版引擎。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>A pure-JS, zero-dependency typesetting engine that outputs bit-perfect PDFs without relying on a browser&#x27;s layout engine. It demonstrates how much you can push in the JS sandbox and opens up offline, deterministic printing workflows. This could inform reproducible publishing pipelines and minimal runtime footprints.</span><span class='zh'>一个纯 JavaScript、零依赖的排版引擎，输出位精确的 PDF，不依赖浏览器排版引擎。展示了在纯 JS 沙箱内还能实现的极致排版能力，并为离线、确定性打印工作流打开了可能性。这样的实现可能推动可重复出版流程和最小化运行时开销的探索。</span></p>

<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/shiehn/sas-audio-processor" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Audio Toolkit for Agents</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：面向代理的音频工具包</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Show HN presents an audio toolkit for agents—modular components for audio input/output, speech processing, and streaming pipelines.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了一个面向代理的音频工具包，提供音频输入/输出、语音处理和流式处理等模块化组件。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Show HN presents an audio toolkit for agents—modular components for audio input/output, speech processing, and streaming pipelines. It demonstrates how agents can reason over real-time audio data and integrate with external services to enhance interactive workflows.</span><span class='zh'>Show HN 展示了一个面向代理的音频工具包，提供音频输入/输出、语音处理和流式处理等模块化组件。展示了代理如何基于实时音频数据进行推理并接入外部服务，提升交互工作流的能力。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>SplatHash provides a compact, fast way to generate placeholder representations for images with minimal computation.</span><span class='zh'>SplatHash 提供一种紧凑、快速的方式来生成图片的占位表示，计算成本极低。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>SplatHash provides a compact, fast way to generate placeholder representations for images with minimal computation. It aims to be simpler and smaller than BlurHash or ThumbHash, making it easier to implement in low-resource apps. This leads to snappier placeholders on mobile and web.</span><span class='zh'>SplatHash 提供一种紧凑、快速的方式来生成图片的占位表示，计算成本极低。它力求比 BlurHash 或 ThumbHash 更简单、占用更小，便于在资源受限的应用中实现。这样可以使移动端和网页上的占位图更流畅。</span></p>

<div class="sbody"><div class="rk">480</div>
<h3 class="st"><a href="https://github.com/mksglu/claude-context-mode" target="_blank" rel="noopener"><span class='en'>Show HN: Context Mode – 315 KB of MCP output becomes 5.4 KB in Claude Code</span><span class='zh'>Show HN：Context Mode—315 KB 的 MCP 输出在 Claude Code 中压缩为 5.4 KB</span></a><span class="dom">github.com</span></h3>
<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>Context Mode demonstrates dramatic output compression in Claude Code, shrinking 315 KB of MCP output down to 5.4 KB while preserving essential context.</span><span class='zh'>Context Mode 展示了在 Claude Code 中对 MCP 输出的显著压缩，将 315 KB 的输出压缩至 5.4 KB，同时保留关键上下文。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>Context Mode demonstrates dramatic output compression in Claude Code, shrinking 315 KB of MCP output down to 5.4 KB while preserving essential context. This signals a breakthrough for memory-heavy coding tasks, enabling leaner contexts and potential speedups in code reasoning and generation.</span><span class='zh'>Context Mode 展示了在 Claude Code 中对 MCP 输出的显著压缩，将 315 KB 的输出压缩至 5.4 KB，同时保留关键上下文。这一做法对需要大量上下文的代码任务具有重要意义，能降低内存占用并提升推理效率。</span></p>

<p class="sum one"><span class='en'>FCaptcha proposes a modern CAPTCHA system with enhanced bot-detection capabilities and adaptive challenges designed to outpace automated solvers.</span><span class='zh'>FCaptcha 提出一种现代 CAPTCHA 体系，结合强化的机器人检测能力与自适应挑战，旨在提升对自动化破解的防护效果。</span></p>
<p class="sum full"><span class='en'>FCaptcha proposes a modern CAPTCHA system with enhanced bot-detection capabilities and adaptive challenges designed to outpace automated solvers.</span><span class='zh'>FCaptcha 提出一种现代 CAPTCHA 体系，结合强化的机器人检测能力与自适应挑战，旨在提升对自动化破解的防护效果。</span></p>
